- **task-stack**: Use before starting any task larger than a one-line fix, when a task is blocked, and when one ships. ([kkucherenkov/shipyard](https://github.com/kkucherenkov/shipyard/tree/HEAD/skills/task-stack))
- **task-stack-capture**: Task capture, run by task-steward in mode capture and by the productivity-worker it dispatches, not for a user request: turns commitments no other job reads (the time study's said-but-not-seen lists, the owner's quick-capture inbox) into one task each in the owner's Insights Portal task stack, never twice. Covers the Run's Steps, the decision for one source item (create, exists, skip or ask), the create op carrying the item's source key, and the section Decide a batch. Not for clarifying existing tasks (task-stack-clarify) or finding done ones (task-stack-reconcile). ([F3-Insights/automation-toolbox](https://github.com/F3-Insights/automation-toolbox/tree/HEAD/productivity/skills/task-stack-capture) · ★1)
- **task-stack-clarify**: The daily GTD clarify pass, run by task-steward (mode clarify) and its productivity-worker; also the method for clarifying one task by hand. Files each open Portal task in its project and domain, rewrites it as a verb-first next action with its owner, date and status, turns delegated work into WAITING with a follow-up date and a named party, and writes one task-stack-apply edit op, never a question. Groups loose tasks sharing a counterparty or thread into a project, opening one when none fits. Covers the Steps and the sections Group tasks into projects and Clarify a batch. Use for "clean up m… ([F3-Insights/automation-toolbox](https://github.com/F3-Insights/automation-toolbox/tree/HEAD/productivity/skills/task-stack-clarify) · ★1)
- **task-stack-produce**: Produces one private, review-ready deliverable for a selected Insights Portal task, project, meeting, email or goal from a source packet the caller gathered: a complete reply draft, a meeting agenda with talking points, replacement text, a worked analysis, or a usable first version. Returns one JSON object, prepared or blocked, and sends, saves and writes nothing. Use when an orchestrator or a person wants the work itself done ahead of the owner rather than a status report about it. Not for an email reply end to end (comms-reply-to-email) or a client-facing document with its own method. ([F3-Insights/automation-toolbox](https://github.com/F3-Insights/automation-toolbox/tree/HEAD/chief-of-staff/skills/task-stack-produce) · ★1)
- **task-stack-reconcile**: The nightly truth pass over the owner's Insights Portal task stack, run by task-steward in mode reconcile and by the productivity-worker it dispatches, not for a user request: closes tasks the evidence shows done, merges duplicates, cancels what the evidence shows overtaken or dropped (never on age alone), never deletes. Covers what to pick, the Run's Steps, and the section Prove a batch (what counts as evidence a task is done). Use for "which of my tasks are already done". Not for filing and retitling (task-stack-clarify) or the weekly review (weekly-review-workstream). ([F3-Insights/automation-toolbox](https://github.com/F3-Insights/automation-toolbox/tree/HEAD/productivity/skills/task-stack-reconcile) · ★1)
- **task-stack-workstream**: Reference loaded after orchestration-workstream (the shared conduct and return block) by the task-stack agents, not for a user request. Covers TASK-STACK-RULES.md first, the Portal read-only, evidence that would convince a person, the owner's own and delegated tasks, proposed writes returned as task-stack-apply ops in extra.ops, the change-set contract every task-stack main agent writes for task-stack-apply, and the reviewer's checklist Check task-stack ops. Read it when writing or changing a task-stack agent. ([F3-Insights/automation-toolbox](https://github.com/F3-Insights/automation-toolbox/tree/HEAD/accounting/skills/task-stack-workstream) · ★1 · 11 copies)
